Number(value) : null;\n}\n\nfunction addMetric(acc, field, value) {\n  const n = toNumberOrNull(value);\n  if (n == null) return;\n  acc[field] = acc[field] == null ? n : acc[field] + n;\n}\n\nfunction formatScalar(value, kind = \"number\") {\n  if (value == null) return (kind === \"cost\" || kind === \"difficulty\") ? \"—\" : \"N/A\";\n  if (kind === \"cost\") return Number(value).toFixed(6);\n  if (kind === \"difficulty\") return Number(value).toFixed(2);\n  return String(value);\n}\n\nfunction asDisplayValue(value, kind = \"number\") {\n  if (kind === \"duration\") return value == null ? \"N/A\" : formatDurationSeconds(value);\n  return formatScalar(value, kind);\n}\n\nfunction phaseLabel(phase) {\n  const idx = parseInt(phase, 10);\n  if (Number.isFinite(idx) && idx >= 0 && idx < VALID_PHASES.length) return VALID_PHASES[idx];\n  return phase;\n}\n\nfunction asCsvValue(value, kind = \"number\") {\n  return formatScalar(value, kind);\n}\n\nfunction phaseOrder(phase) {\n  const idx = VALID_PHASES.indexOf(phase);\n  return idx === -1 ? Infinity : idx;\n}\n\nfunction sortRows(rows) {\n  return [...rows].sort((a, b) => {\n    const pa = phaseOrder(a.phase);\n    const pb = phaseOrder(b.phase);\n    if (pa !== pb) return pa - pb;\n    return a.date.localeCompare(b.date);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction groupRowsByPhase(rows, { dateDesc = false } = {}) {\n  const sorted = sortRows(rows);\n  const groups = new Map();\n  for (const row of sorted) {\n    if (!groups.has(row.phase)) groups.set(row.phase, []);\n    groups.get(row.phase).push(row);\n  }\n  if (dateDesc) {\n    for (const phaseRows of groups.values()) {\n      phaseRows.reverse();\n    }\n  }\n  return groups;\n}\n\nexport function computePhaseSummary(phaseRows) {\n  if (!phaseRows || phaseRows.length === 0) return null;\n  const n = phaseRows.length;\n  let totalCalls = 0;\n  let totalDuration = 0;\n  let totalInput = 0;\n  let totalOutput = 0;\n  let totalCacheRead = 0;\n  let costSum = 0;\n  let costCount = 0;\n  let durationCount = 0;\n\n  for (const row of phaseRows) {\n    totalCalls += row.callCount || 0;\n    if (row.durationMs != null) { totalDuration += row.durationMs; durationCount += 1; }\n    totalInput += row.tokenInput || 0;\n    totalOutput += row.tokenOutput || 0;\n    totalCacheRead += row.cacheRead || 0;\n    if (row.cost != null) { costSum += row.cost; costCount += 1; }\n  }\n\n  const cacheHitRate = (totalInput + totalCacheRead) > 0\n    ? totalCacheRead / (totalInput + totalCacheRead)\n    : null;\n\n  return {\n    totalCalls,\n    avgCost: costCount > 0 ? costSum / costCount : null,\n    avgDuration: durationCount > 0 ? totalDuration / durationCount : null,\n    avgTokenInput: n > 0 ? totalInput / n : null,\n    avgTokenOutput: n > 0 ? totalOutput / n : null,\n    cacheHitRate,\n  };\n}\n\nconst TEXT_MAX_ROWS = 7;\n\nexport function formatText(rows) {\n  if (!rows.length) return \"No metrics data found.\";\n\n  const lines = [];\n  const groups = groupRowsByPhase(rows, { dateDesc: true });\n\n  for (const [phase, allPhaseRows] of groups.entries()) {\n    lines.push(`PHASE ${phaseLabel(phase)}`);\n    lines.push(\"\");\n    lines.push(\"             |             | token          | cache          |            |            |          |\");\n    lines.push(\"             | difficulty  | in      out    | read   create  | call count | cost       | duration |\");\n    lines.push(\"---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\");\n\n    const displayed = allPhaseRows.slice(0, TEXT_MAX_ROWS);\n    const elided = allPhaseRows.length - displayed.length;\n\n    for (const row of displayed) {\n      const incomplete = row.costIncomplete && row.cost != null;\n      const date = row.date.padEnd(11, \" \");\n      const diff = asDisplayValue(row.difficulty, \"difficulty\").padEnd(11, \" \");\n      const inTok = asDisplayValue(row.tokenInput).padEnd(7, \" \");\n      const outTok = asDisplayValue(row.tokenOutput).padEnd(7, \" \");\n      const read = asDisplayValue(row.cacheRead).padEnd(6, \" \");\n      const create = asDisplayValue(row.cacheCreate).padEnd(7, \" \");\n      const calls = asDisplayValue(row.callCount).padEnd(10, \" \");\n      const suffix = incomplete ? \" +\" : \"\";\n      const cost = (asDisplayValue(row.cost, \"cost\") + suffix).padEnd(10, \" \");\n      const duration = asDisplayValue(row.durationMs, \"duration\");\n      lines.push(`${date} | ${diff} | ${inTok} ${outTok} | ${read} ${create} | ${calls} | ${cost} | ${duration}`);\n    }\n    if (elided > 0) {\n      lines.push(`... and ${elided} more`);\n    }\n\n    const summary = computePhaseSummary(allPhaseRows);\n    if (summary) {\n      lines.push(\"---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\");\n      const avgCostStr = summary.avgCost != null ? `$${summary.avgCost.toFixed(4)}` : \"—\";\n      const avgDurStr = summary.avgDuration != null ? formatDurationSeconds(summary.avgDuration) : \"—\";\n      const cacheStr = summary.cacheHitRate != null ? `${(summary.cacheHitRate * 100).toFixed(0)}%` : \"—\";\n      lines.push(`  avg cost: ${avgCostStr} | avg duration: ${avgDurStr} | total calls: ${summary.totalCalls} | avg tokens: ${Math.round(summary.avgTokenInput || 0)}/${Math.round(summary.avgTokenOutput || 0)} | cache hit: ${cacheStr}`);\n    }\n    lines.push(\"\");\n  }\n\n  return lines.join(\"\\n\").trimEnd();\n}\n\nexport function formatJson(rows) {\n  const groups = groupRowsByPhase(rows);\n  const phaseSummary = {};\n  for (const [phase, phaseRows] of groups.entries()) {\n    phaseSummary[phase] = computePhaseSummary(phaseRows);\n  }\n  const sorted = sortRows(rows);\n  return JSON.stringify({ rows: sorted, phaseSummary }, null, 2);\n}\n\nexport function formatCsv(rows) {\n  const header = \"date,phase,difficulty,tokenInput,tokenOutput,cacheRead,cacheCreate,callCount,cost,durationMs,incomplete\";\n  const lines = [header];\n  const groups = groupRowsByPhase(rows);\n\n  for (const [phase, phaseRows] of groups.entries()) {\n    for (const row of phaseRows) {\n      lines.push([\n        row.date,\n        phaseLabel(row.phase),\n        asCsvValue(row.difficulty, \"difficulty\"),\n        asCsvValue(row.tokenInput),\n        asCsvValue(row.tokenOutput),\n        asCsvValue(row.cacheRead),\n        asCsvValue(row.cacheCreate),\n        asCsvValue(row.callCount),\n        asCsvValue(row.cost, \"cost\"),\n        asCsvValue(row.durationMs),\n        row.costIncomplete && row.cost != null ? \"+\" : \"\",\n      ].join(\",\"));\n    }\n    const summary = computePhaseSummary(phaseRows);\n    if (summary) {\n      lines.push([\n        \"SUMMARY\",\n        phaseLabel(phase),\n        \"\",\n        summary.avgTokenInput != null ? Math.round(summary.avgTokenInput) : \"\",\n        summary.avgTokenOutput != null ? Math.round(summary.avgTokenOutput) : \"\",\n        \"\",\n        \"\",\n        summary.totalCalls,\n        summary.avgCost != null ? summary.avgCost.toFixed(6) : \"—\",\n        summary.avgDuration != null ? Math.round(summary.avgDuration) : \"\",\n        \"\",\n      ].join(\",\"));\n    }\n  }\n  return lines.join(\"\\n\");\n}\n\nasync function listFlowFiles(specsDir) {\n  const files = [];\n  const stack = [specsDir];\n\n  while (stack.length > 0) {\n    const dir = stack.pop();\n    const entries = await fs.readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true });\n    for (const entry of entries) {\n      if (entry.isSymbolicLink()) continue;\n      const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);\n      if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n        stack.push(fullPath);\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

addMetric(row, \"cacheRead\", tokens.cacheRead);\n  addMetric(row, \"cacheCreate\", tokens.cacheCreation);\n  addMetric(row, \"callCount\", phaseData.callCount);\n  addMetric(row, \"cost\", };\n}\n\nasync function safeReadJson(filePath) {\n  try {\n    const text = await fs.readFile(filePath, \"utf8\");\n    return JSON.parse(text);\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nasync toNumberOrNull(reviewCount[k]));\n  if (values.some((v) => v == null)) return null;\n  return values[0] + values[1] + values[2];\n}\n\nfunction computeRequirementCount(spec) {\n  if The metric name specMdChars is retained for historical series\n  // continuity. Historic specs without spec.json (pre-T11) are skipped via\n  // returning null — metrics.token iterates many past flows and must tolerate\n  // absent artifacts (spec 207 R2 carve-out for multi-spec iteration).\n  //   Malformed spec.json still throws via JSON.parse.\n  const specJsonPath = path.join(specDir, \"spec.json\");\n  let stat;\n  try {\n    stat = await fs.stat(specJsonPath);\n  } catch (err) {\n    if (err.code !== \"ENOENT\") throw err;\n    // Historical spec without spec.json — skip this entry rather than failing\n    // the whole metrics run. (required.some((v) => v == null)) return null;\n  if (requestChars <= 0) return null;\n\n  const baseDifficulty = average([\n    normalizeToHundred(specMdChars, DIFFICULTY_BASELINES.specMdChars),\n    normalizeToHundred(requirementCount, DIFFICULTY_BASELINES.requirementCount),\n    normalizeToHundred(testCount, DIFFICULTY_BASELINES.testCount),\n    normalizeToHundred(reviewCount, entry of metrics) {\n    if (!entry || !entry.phase || entry.kind !== \"agent\") continue;\n    const p = byPhase[entry.phase] = byPhase[entry.phase] || {};\n    p.callCount = (p.callCount || 0) + (entry.callCount || 0);\n    if (entry.durationMs != null) p.durationMs = (p.durationMs || 0) + entry.durationMs;\n    if (entry.tokens) {\n      p.tokens = p.tokens || { input: 0, output: 0, cacheRead: 0, cacheCreation: 0 };\n      for (const k of [\"input\", \"output\", \"cacheRead\", \"cacheCreation\"]) {\n        p.tokens[k] += entry.tokens[k] || 0;\n      }\n    }\n    if (entry.cost != null) p.cost = (p.cost || 0) + entry.cost;\n    if (entry.cost == null || entry.cost === 0) p.costIncomplete = true;\n  }\n  return byPhase;\n}\n\nasync function buildRows(flowEntries) {\n  const rows = new Map();\n  for (const entry of flowEntries) {\n    const { path: filePath, parsed } = entry;\n    const date = isoDateFromFinalizedAt(parsed?.state?.finalizedAt);\n    if (!date) {\n      process.stderr.write(`sdd-forge metrics token: skipping ${filePath} — missing state.finalizedAt\\n`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    const specDir = path.dirname(filePath);\n    const specDifficulty = await computeSpecDifficulty(parsed, specDir);\n    const metrics = parsed?.metrics;\n    if (!metrics || typeof metrics !== \"object\") continue;\n\n    const phaseMap = normalizeMetrics(metrics);\n    for (const [phase, phaseData] of Object.entries(phaseMap)) {\n      if (!phaseData || typeof phaseData !== \"object\") continue;\n      const key = toRowKey(date, phase);\n      if (!rows.has(key)) rows.set(key, createEmptyRow(date, phase));\n      applyPhaseMetrics(rows.get(key), phaseData, specDifficulty);\n    }\n  }\n  return sortRows([...rows.values()].map(finalizeRow));\n}\n\nasync function readCacheRows(cachePath) {\n  const text = await fs.readFile(cachePath, \"utf8\");\n  const parsed = JSON.parse(text);\n  if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.rows)) {\n    throw new Error(`invalid cache format: ${cachePath}`);\n  }\n  return sortRows(parsed.rows);\n}\n\nasync function writeCache(cachePath, rows, maxFinalizedAt) {\n  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(cachePath), { recursive: true });\n  const payload = {\n    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    maxFinalizedAt,\n    rows: sortRows(rows),\n  };\n  await fs.writeFile(cachePath, `${JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)}\\n`, \"utf8\");\n}\n\nfunction render(rows, format) {\n  if (format === \"json\") return formatJson(rows);\n  if (format === \"csv\") return formatCsv(rows);\n  return formatText(rows);\n}\n\nasync function runToken(rawArgs, container) {\n  let opts;\n  try {\n    opts = parseArgs(rawArgs, {\n      options: [\"--format\"],\n      defaults: { format: DEFAULT_FORMAT },\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    usageError(err.message);\n  }\n\n  if (opts.help) {\n    process.stdout.write(`${formatUsage()}\\n`);\n    process.exit(EXIT_SUCCESS);\n  }\n  const format = String(opts.format || DEFAULT_FORMAT).toLowerCase();\n  if (!SUPPORTED_FORMATS.has(format)) {\n    usageError(`Unknown format: ${format}`);\n  }\n\n  const root = container.get(\"root\");\n  const specsDir = path.join(root, \"specs\");\n  const cachePath = path.join(root, \".sdd-forge\", \"output\", \"metrics.json\");\n\n  let specsStat;\n  try {\n    specsStat = await fs.stat(specsDir);\n  } catch (err) {\n    throw new Error(`specs directory not found: ${specsDir}`);\n  }\n  if (!specsStat.isDirectory()) throw new Error(`specs path is not a directory: ${specsDir}`);\n\n  const flowFiles = await listFlowFiles(specsDir);\n  const flowEntries = [];\n  for (const file of flowFiles) {\n    const text = await fs.readFile(file.path, \"utf8\");\n    let parsed;\n    try {\n      parsed = JSON.parse(text);\n    } catch (err) {\n      throw new Error(`invalid JSON in ${file.path}: ${err.message}`);\n    }\n    flowEntries.push({ path: file.path, parsed });\n  }\n  const maxFinalizedAt = computeMaxFinalizedAt(flowEntries);\n  let rows;\n  const canReuseCache = await isCacheFresh(cachePath, maxFinalizedAt);\n  if (canReuseCache) {\n    rows = await readCacheRows(cachePath);\n  } else {\n    rows = await buildRows(flowEntries);\n    await writeCache(cachePath, rows, maxFinalizedAt);\n  }\n\n  process.stdout.write(`${render(rows, format)}\\n`);\n}\n\nexport default class TokenCommand extends Command {\n  static outputMode = \"raw\";\n  async execute(ctx) {\n    return runToken(ctx._rawArgs || [], ctx.container);\n  }\n}\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_15","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,260p' tests/unit/metrics/token-duration.test.js\"","aggregated_output":"/**\n * tests/unit/metrics/token-duration.test.js\n *\n * Tests for duration display in `sdd-forge metrics token` output (spec 191 R4).\n * Verifies that per-phase duration is rendered in seconds (one decimal) in\n * the text and included in CSV/JSON rows.\n */\n\nimport { describe, it } from \"node:test\";\nimport assert from \"node:assert/strict\";\nimport { formatText, formatCsv, buildRowsFromMetrics } from \"../../../src/metrics/commands/token.js\";\n\ndescribe(\"metrics token: duration display (spec 191 R4)\", () => {\n  it(\"buildRowsFromMetrics extracts durationMs per phase-date row\", () => {\n    const rows = buildRowsFromMetrics(\"2026-04-18\", {\n      draft: {\n        tokens: { input: 100, output: 50, cacheRead: 0, cacheCreation: 0 },\n        cost: 0.001,\n        callCount: 2,\n        durationMs: 12300,\n      },\n      spec: {\n        tokens: { input: 50, output: 25, cacheRead: 0, cacheCreation: 0 },\n        cost: 0.0005,\n        callCount: 1,\n        durationMs: 4100,\n      },\n    });\n\n    const byPhase = Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.phase, r]));\n    assert.equal(byPhase.draft.durationMs, 12300);\n    assert.equal(byPhase.spec.durationMs, 4100);\n  });\n\n  it(\"formatText includes a duration column with seconds (one decimal)\", () => {\n    const rows = [\n      {\n        date: \"2026-04-18\", phase: \"draft\", difficulty: null,\n        tokenInput: 100, tokenOutput: 50, cacheRead: 0, cacheCreate: 0,\n        callCount: 2, cost: 0.001, durationMs: 12300,\n      },\n    ];\n    const text = formatText(rows);\n    assert.ok(/duration/i.test(text), `header should mention duration; got:\\n${text}`);\n    assert.ok(text.includes(\"12.3s\"), `row should show 12.3s; got:\\n${text}`);\n  });\n\n  it(\"formatText shows N/A when durationMs is null\", () => {\n    const rows = [\n      {\n        date: \"2026-04-18\", phase: \"draft\", difficulty: null,\n        tokenInput: 100, tokenOutput: 50, cacheRead: 0, cacheCreate: 0,\n        callCount: 2, cost: 0.001, durationMs: null,\n      },\n    ];\n    const text = formatText(rows);\n    assert.ok(text.includes(\"N/A\"), \"N/A should appear when durationMs is null\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"formatCsv includes durationMs column\", () => {\n    const rows = [\n      {\n        date: \"2026-04-18\", phase: \"draft\", difficulty: null,\n        tokenInput: 100, tokenOutput: 50, cacheRead: 0, cacheCreate: 0,\n        callCount: 2, cost: 0.001, durationMs: 12300,\n      },\n    ];\n    const csv = formatCsv(rows);\n    const [header, row] = csv.split(\"\\n\");\n    assert.ok(header.split(\",\").includes(\"durationMs\"), `CSV header should include durationMs; got: ${header}`);\n    assert.ok(row.endsWith(\",12300\") || row.includes(\",12300,\") || row.includes(\",12300\\n\") || /,12300$/.test(row), `CSV row should include 12300; got: ${row}`);\n  });\n});\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_17","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,240p' tests/helpers/metrics-token.js\"","aggregated_output":"import { join } from \"node:path\";\nimport { execFileSync, spawnSync } from \"node:child_process\";\nimport { writeJson } from \"./tmp-dir.js\";\n\nexport const SDD_FORGE = join(process.cwd(), \"src/sdd-forge.js\");\n\nexport function writeBaseConfig(tmp) {\n  writeJson(tmp, \".sdd-forge/config.json\", {\n    lang: \"ja\",\n    type: \"base\",\n    docs: { languages: [\"ja\"], defaultLanguage: \"ja\" },\n  });\n}\n\nexport function runToken(tmp, args = []) {\n  return execFileSync(\"node\", [SDD_FORGE, \"metrics\", \"token\", ...args], {\n    encoding: \"utf8\",\n    env: { ...process.env, SDD_FORGE_WORK_ROOT: tmp, SDD_FORGE_SOURCE_ROOT: tmp },\n    cwd: tmp,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function runTokenJson(tmp) {\n  return runToken(tmp, [\"--format\", \"json\"]);\n}\n\nexport function runTokenCapture(tmp, args = [\"--format\", \"json\"]) {\n  const res = spawnSync(\"node\", [SDD_FORGE, \"metrics\", \"token\", ...args], {\n    encoding: \"utf8\",\n    env: { ...process.env, SDD_FORGE_WORK_ROOT: tmp, SDD_FORGE_SOURCE_ROOT: tmp },\n    cwd: tmp,\n  });\n  return { stdout: res.stdout ?? \"\", stderr: res.stderr ?? \"\", status: res.status };\n}\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

{\n  const flow = {\n    metrics: {\n      draft: {\n        tokens: { input: 100, output: 50, cacheRead: 20, cacheCreation: 10 },\n        cost: 0.01,\n        callCount: 2,\n      },\n    },\n  };\n  if (finalizedAt !== undefined) {\n    flow.state = { finalizedAt };\n  }\n  return flow;\n}\n\ndescribe(\"metrics token — state.finalizedAt as date axis (R2, R3, R4, R5)\", () => {\n  let tmp;\n  afterEach(() => tmp && removeTmpDir(tmp));\n\n  it(\"R2: uses state.finalizedAt as the date column (not file mtime)\", () => {\n    tmp = createTmpDir(\"sdd-metrics-finalized-at-\");\n    writeBaseConfig(tmp);\n    writeJson(\n      tmp,\n      \"specs/001-alpha/flow.json\",\n      metricsFlow(\"2025-06-15T12:00:00Z\"),\n    );\n\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(runTokenJson(tmp));\n    const row = parsed.rows.find((r) => r.phase === \"draft\");\n    assert.ok(row, \"draft row should exist\");\n    assert.equal(row.date, \"2025-06-15\", \"date axis should come from finalizedAt\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"R3: skips specs missing state.finalizedAt and prints a warning\", () => {\n    tmp = createTmpDir(\"sdd-metrics-finalized-at-missing-\");\n    writeBaseConfig(tmp);\n    writeJson(tmp, \"specs/001-alpha/flow.json\", metricsFlow());\n    writeJson(\n      tmp,\n      \"specs/002-beta/flow.json\",\n      metricsFlow(\"2025-06-20T00:00:00Z\"),\n    );\n\n    const res = runTokenCapture(tmp);\n    assert.equal(res.status, 0, `expected exit 0, got ${res.status}. stderr=${res.stderr}`);\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(res.stdout);\n    const draftRows = parsed.rows.filter((r) => r.phase === \"draft\");\n    assert.equal(draftRows.length, 1, \"only specs with finalizedAt should be aggregated\");\n    assert.equal(draftRows[0].date, \"2025-06-20\");\n    assert.match(\n      res.stderr,\n      /finalizedAt/i,\n      \"warning about missing finalizedAt should be emitted on stderr\",\n    );\n  });\n\n  it(\"R4/R5: cache without maxFinalizedAt is invalidated and rebuilt\", () => {\n    tmp = createTmpDir(\"sdd-metrics-finalized-at-cache-\");\n    writeBaseConfig(tmp);\n    writeJson(\n      tmp,\n      \"specs/001-alpha/flow.json\",\n      metricsFlow(\"2025-06-15T12:00:00Z\"),\n    );\n    writeJson(tmp, \".sdd-forge/output/metrics.json\", {\n      generatedAt: \"2020-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\n      rows: [\n        {\n          date: \"2020-01-01\",\n          phase: \"draft\",\n          difficulty: null,\n          tokenInput: 999999,\n          tokenOutput: 0,\n          cacheRead: 0,\n          cacheCreate: 0,\n          callCount: 0,\n          cost: 0,\n        },\n      ],\n    });\n\n    runTokenJson(tmp);\n\n    const cachePath = join(tmp, \".sdd-forge/output/metrics.json\");\n    const cache = JSON.parse(readFileSync(cachePath, \"utf8\"));\n    assert.ok(\n      typeof cache.maxFinalizedAt === \"string\" && cache.maxFinalizedAt.length > 0,\n      \"cache should contain maxFinalizedAt after rebuild\",\n    );\n    assert.equal(\n      cache.rows[0].tokenInput,\n      100,\n      \"legacy cache rows should be replaced with fresh aggregation\",\n    );\n    assert.equal(cache.rows[0].date, \"2025-06-15\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"R4: cache is reused when maxFinalizedAt matches current max\", () => {\n    tmp = createTmpDir(\"sdd-metrics-finalized-at-cache-reuse-\");\n    writeBaseConfig(tmp);\n    writeJson(\n      tmp,\n      \"specs/001-alpha/flow.json\",\n      metricsFlow(\"2025-06-15T12:00:00Z\"),\n    );\n    writeJson(tmp, \".sdd-forge/output/metrics.json\", {\n      generatedAt: \"2025-06-16T00:00:00Z\",\n      maxFinalizedAt: \"2025-06-15T12:00:00Z\",\n      rows: [\n        {\n          date: \"2025-06-15\",\n          phase: \"draft\",\n          difficulty: null,\n          tokenInput: 42,\n          tokenOutput: 0,\n          cacheRead: 0,\n          cacheCreate: 0,\n          callCount: 0,\n          cost: 0,\n        },\n      ],\n    });\n\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(runTokenJson(tmp));\n    const row = parsed.rows.find((r) => r.phase === \"draft\");\n    assert.equal(row.tokenInput, 42, \"cached row should be returned as-is\");\n  });\n});\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

process.stderr.write(`${message}\\\\n`);\\n  process.stderr.write(`${formatUsage()}\\\\n`);\\n  process.exit(EXIT_ERROR);\\n}\\n\\nfunction isoDateFromFinalizedAt(iso) {\\n  if (typeof iso !== \\\"string\\\") return null;\\n  if (!/^\\\\d{4}-\\\\d{2}-\\\\d{2}T/.test(iso)) return null;\\n  return iso.slice(0, 10);\\n}\\n\\nfunction toNumberOrNull(value) {\\n  return Number.isFinite(value) ? Number(value) : null;\\n}\\n\\nfunction addMetric(acc, field, value) {\\n  const n = toNumberOrNull(value);\\n  if (n == null) return;\\n  acc[field] = acc[field] == null ? n : acc[field] + n;\\n}\\n\\nfunction formatScalar(value, kind = \\\"number\\\") {\\n  if (value == null) return (kind === \\\"cost\\\" || kind === \\\"difficulty\\\") ? \\\"—\\\" : \\\"N/A\\\";\\n  if (kind === \\\"cost\\\") return Number(value).toFixed(6);\\n  if (kind === \\\"difficulty\\\") return Number(value).toFixed(2);\\n  return String(value);\\n}\\n\\nfunction asDisplayValue(value, kind = \\\"number\\\") {\\n  if (kind === \\\"duration\\\") return value == null ? \\\"N/A\\\" : formatDurationSeconds(value);\\n  return formatScalar(value, kind);\\n}\\n\\nfunction phaseLabel(phase) {\\n  const idx = parseInt(phase, 10);\\n  if (Number.isFinite(idx) && idx >= 0 && idx < VALID_PHASES.length) return VALID_PHASES[idx];\\n  return phase;\\n}\\n\\nfunction asCsvValue(value, kind = \\\"number\\\") {\\n  return formatScalar(value, kind);\\n}\\n\\nfunction phaseOrder(phase) {\\n  const idx = VALID_PHASES.indexOf(phase);\\n  return idx === -1 ? Infinity : idx;\\n}\\n\\nfunction sortRows(rows) {\\n  return [...rows].sort((a, b) => {\\n    const pa = phaseOrder(a.phase);\\n    const pb = phaseOrder(b.phase);\\n    if (pa !== pb) return pa - pb;\\n    return a.date.localeCompare(b.date);\\n  });\\n}\\n\\nfunction groupRowsByPhase(rows, { dateDesc = false } = {}) {\\n  const sorted = sortRows(rows);\\n  const groups = new Map();\\n  for (const row of sorted) {\\n    if (!groups.has(row.phase)) groups.set(row.phase, []);\\n    groups.get(row.phase).push(row);\\n  }\\n  if (dateDesc) {\\n    for (const phaseRows of groups.values()) {\\n      phaseRows.reverse();\\n    }\\n  }\\n  return groups;\\n}\\n\\nexport function computePhaseSummary(phaseRows) {\\n  if (!phaseRows || phaseRows.length === 0) return null;\\n  const n = phaseRows.length;\\n  let totalCalls = 0;\\n  let totalDuration = 0;\\n  let totalInput = 0;\\n  let totalOutput = 0;\\n  let totalCacheRead = 0;\\n  let costSum = 0;\\n  let costCount = 0;\\n  let durationCount = 0;\\n\\n  for (const row of phaseRows) {\\n    totalCalls += row.callCount || 0;\\n    if (row.durationMs != null) { totalDuration += row.durationMs; durationCount += 1; }\\n    totalInput += row.tokenInput || 0;\\n    totalOutput += row.tokenOutput || 0;\\n    totalCacheRead += row.cacheRead || 0;\\n    if (row.cost != null) { costSum += row.cost; costCount += 1; }\\n  }\\n\\n  const cacheHitRate = (totalInput + totalCacheRead) > 0\\n    ? `sdd-forge docs build --verbose --force`, `sdd-forge docs changelog --dry-run`, `sdd-forge docs forge --prompt-file prompt.md --mode agent --max-runs 3`.\\\\n\\\\n#### `sdd-forge flow`\\\\nUsage: `sdd-forge flow <prepare|resume|get|set|run> ...`.\\\\nAliases: `flow prepare` routes to `run prepare-spec`; `flow resume` routes to `run resume`.\\\\nExamples: `sdd-forge flow get check dirty`, `sdd-forge flow set step approval done`, `sdd-forge flow run review --phase spec`, `sdd-forge flow run tests --baseline`.\\\\n\\\\n#### `sdd-forge metrics`\\\\nUsage: `sdd-forge metrics <command>`.\\\\nBehavior: registry-dispatched metrics commands; help path shows available keys.\\\\nExample: `sdd-forge metrics --help` and `sdd-forge metrics token` (listed in help layout).\\\\n\\\\n#### `sdd-forge spec`\\\\nUsage: `sdd-forge spec <command> [options]`.\\\\nKnown analyzed subcommand: `render` with `--spec <dir>` and `--out <path>`; validates schema before writing Markdown.\\\\nExample: `sdd-forge spec render --spec specs/001-example --out specs/001-example/spec.md`.\\\\n\\\\n#### `sdd-forge help`\\\\nUsage: `sdd-forge help`.\\\\nBehavior: prints localized grouped command list with package version and per-command descriptions.\\\\nExample: `sdd-forge help`.\\\\n<!-- {{/text}} -->\\\\n\\\\n### Exit Codes and Unambiguous Requirements — 4つの変更は具体的な条件（`data.tests === null` 時に `- ` を表示する等）で記述されており、曖昧な形容詞はない; Complete Context — 各要件にトリガー条件（現在の挙動）と期待動作（変更後）が明記されている; No Hardcoded Secrets — ソースコードの秘密情報に関する内容は含まれず、該当なし; Prioritize Requirements — 4つの改善項目が列挙されているが、優先順位が明示されていない（Q1 で \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"4 concrete improvements\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" と記載されているが、順序・優先度の根拠がない）; Impact on Existing Features — Q2 にて既存の呼び出し元・テスト・データ構造への影響が明示されている; Present Recommendations with Reasoning — 各Q&Aで推奨案と根拠（例：Q6の代替案却下理由）が示されている; Explore Before Asking — Q2・Q4でコードを調査した上で回答しており（`generateReport()` の行番号、`data.tests` の導出元等）、ユーザーへの確認前に調査が行われている; Proactively Raise Related Implications — Q4でエッジケース、Q7で将来の影響が先回りして記載されている; Confirm Brainstorm vs. Decision Before Critiquing — ドラフトは決定事項として記述されており、ブレインストーミング/決定の混同は見られない; Capture Digressions in draft.md — 脱線したトピックは見当たらず、該当なし; Draft Stays at Requirements Level — 実装詳細（関数設計等）は含まれず、要件レベルに留まっている; otherwise it shall reuse existing cache.\\\\n../specs/166-cache-hit-ratio-aggregation/issue-log.json:55:      \\\\\\\"reason\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"P1 — R1 (standalone top-level command) — `sdd-forge.js` adds `metrics` to namespace dispatchers; `metrics.js` routes to `token` subcommand independently without requiring `check`.; P1 — R2 (read only specs/*/flow.json) — `listFlowFiles()` scans `specs/` directory for `flow.json` files only; no `.tmp/logs` path referenced.; P1 — R3 (--format text|json|csv, text default) — `DEFAULT_FORMAT = \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"`, `SUPPORTED_FORMATS = new Set([\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"json\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"csv\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"])`, all three renderers produce same aggregation fields.; P1 — R4 (text output: phase blocks, date rows, columns difficulty/tokenIn/tokenOut/cacheRead/cacheCreate/callCount/cost) — `formatText()` emits `PHASE <name>` headers, per-date rows, and all seven required columns.; P1 — R5 (missing fields render as N/A) — `createEmptyRow()` initializes all fields to `null`; `asDisplayValue(null)` returns `\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"N/A\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"`; processing continues.; P1 — R6 (runtime failure → non-zero exit) — `main().catch()` calls `process.exit(EXIT_ERROR)`; JSON parse errors, unreadable files, and cache write failures all throw and are caught.; P1 — R7 (5000 file bound, non-zero exit on overflow) — `MAX_FLOW_FILES = 5000`; `listFlowFiles()` throws when `files.length > MAX_FLOW_FILES`, which propagates to `EXIT_ERROR`.; P2 — R8 (cache freshness: regenerate when any flow.json newer than metrics.json) — `isCacheFresh()` compares cache mtime against the *latest* flow.json mtime, not against each individual file. If the cache was written after the newest flow.json, it returns true and reuses the cache. This is correct behavior for the spec's stated rule (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"newer than `.sdd-forge/output/metrics.json`\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"), so this is actually a PASS.; P2 — R8 (cache freshness check) — `isCacheFresh()` compares `cachePath` mtime against `latestFlowMtimeMs` (max of all flow.json mtimes); cache is regenerated when missing or older than any source file.\\\\\\\",\\\\n../specs/166-cache-hit-ratio-aggregation/flow.json:129:      \\\\\\\"desc\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"R8: metrics.json キャッシュの鮮度判定で再生成/再利用\\\\\\\",\\\\n../specs/166-cache-hit-ratio-aggregation/flow.json:174:      \\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"draft: metricsキャッシュ再生成は specs/*/flow.json と output/metrics.json のmtime比較のみ\\\\\\\",\\\\n../specs/239-retro-incomplete-reasons/issue.md:1:When retro rate < 1.0, append the requirement IDs and notes for partial/not_done items to the end of the Retro section in the report. 1. Unit test coverage is overlooked\\\\n**File:** `tests/unit/metrics/token-duration.test.js`  \\\\n**Issue:** This test imports `formatText`, `formatCsv`, and `buildRowsFromMetrics`, and asserts the old table/CSV behavior. The spec only mentions `token.test.js`, so this test will likely fail or miss new `specCount` / `cacheHitRate` behavior.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Add explicit spec/test tasks to update this unit test for the new global header, AVG row layout, CSV columns, cost precision, and `buildRowsFromMetrics` returning `specCount=1` plus `cacheHitRate`.\\\\n\\\\n### 2. Cache reuse test contradicts new cache-version behavior\\\\n**File:** `tests/e2e/metrics/token-finalized-at.test.js`  \\\\n**Issue:** The existing cache reuse test creates a cache with matching `maxFinalizedAt` and expects it to be reused as-is. With mandatory new row fields, reusing an unversioned old cache would omit `specCount` and `cacheHitRate`. R7 is only `should`, but the new fields are `must`.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Make cache version invalidation a `must`, and require tests where unversioned/mismatched-version cache rebuilds even when `maxFinalizedAt` matches. Update the reuse fixture to include the expected cache `version` and new row fields.\\\\n\\\\n### 3. Exported helper path is missing from the module list\\\\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \\\\n**Issue:** The spec lists `buildRows`, `createEmptyRow`, `applyPhaseMetrics`, and `finalizeRow`, but omits exported `buildRowsFromMetrics()`. That helper is used by tests and currently finalizes rows directly, so it must also produce `specCount` and `cacheHitRate`.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Add `buildRowsFromMetrics` to the Scope/Modules/Data Flow and specify its expected behavior for single-flow rows.\\\\n\\\\n### 4. Cache read path is underspecified\\\\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \\\\n**Issue:** The spec mentions `writeCache` and `isCacheFresh`, but not `readCacheRows`. If cache validation is incomplete, `readCacheRows` can still return old rows missing the new required fields.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Add a requirement that cached rows are only read after schema/version validation, or that `readCacheRows` rejects caches missing required row fields.\\\\n\\\\n### 5. Tracked generated cache artifact is not addressed\\\\n**File:** `.sdd-forge/output/metrics.json`  \\\\n**Issue:** This file is tracked and currently contains old row objects without `version`, `specCount`, or `cacheHitRate`. The spec changes the cache schema but does not say whether this artifact must be regenerated, updated, or intentionally left untouched.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Add an explicit decision for the tracked cache artifact: regenerate it with the new schema, remove it from the change scope, or document why it is excluded.\\\\n\\\\n### 6. Summary behavior across CSV/JSON is ambiguous\\\\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \\\\n**Issue:** `computePhaseSummary()` feeds both JSON `phaseSummary` and CSV summary rows, but R8 only says `avgSpecCount` and `avgDifficulty` are reflected in the AVG row. It does not clearly define whether CSV `SUMMARY` becomes `AVG`, whether CSV includes `avgSpecCount`, `avgDifficulty`, `avgCallCount`, or whether JSON `phaseSummary` must change.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Specify the summary contract per format: text AVG row columns, CSV summary row label/columns, and JSON `phaseSummary` fields including `avgCallCount`, `avgSpecCount`, `avgDifficulty`, and weighted `cacheHitRate`.\\\"}}\\nspecs/247-metrics-token-avg-row/spec.json:2:  \\\"goal\\\": \\\"metrics token の出力を改修し、サマリー行を AVG 行としてテーブルカラムに揃えて表示する。specs 列・cache hit 列を全フォーマットに追加し、cost 精度を toFixed(1) Context — QA 各エントリに evidence・why・considered が揃い、独立した2人のレビュアーが実装の合否を判断できる水準に達している。; No Hardcoded Secrets — フォーマット変更のみの spec であり、シークレット類は一切含まれない。; Prioritize Requirements — scopeVerification.in に5項目の要件があるが、must / should / nice-to-have の優先度が一切割り当てられていない。3項目超の場合は優先度付けが必須。; Impact on Existing Features — impactOnExisting に3項目（外部ツール影響・token.test.js アサーション破損・CSV アサーション影響）が明示されている。; Present Recommendations with Reasoning — 全 QA エントリに推奨回答 + evidence（コード箇所引用）+ why + considered の構成が整っている。; Explore Before Asking — token.js の具体的な行番号（:113-146, :284-307, :159-161 等）を参照しており、コードを事前に読んで回答していることが確認できる。; Proactively Raise Related Implications — キャッシュスキーマ変更による旧キャッシュ不整合、e2e テスト破損、CSV アサーション影響など関連する波及を事前に列挙している。; Confirm Brainstorm vs. Decision Before Critiquing — approval.approved=true で承認済みの決定ドキュメントであり、ブレインストーム段階ではな��。; Capture Digressions in draft.md — これは構造化された spec であり、会話上の脱線が発生する文脈ではないため評価不可。; Draft Stays at Requirements Level — キャッシュ再計算の QA 回答（answer フィールド）に「version フィールドを追加しコード側の期待 version と不一致ならリビルド」という内部データ構造・制御フローの記述が含まれている。answer フィールドは guardrail の除外対象（evidence/why/considered）ではなく、要件レベルの記述（例:「旧フォーマット検出時に自動再構築すること」）に留めるべき。; Backward-Compatible CLI Interface — CLI コマンド・オプションの変更はなく、変更対象は formatText の出力フォーマットのみ。マイグレーションプランの適用外。\\\",\\nspecs/247-metrics-token-avg-row/draft.json:3:  \\\"goal\\\": \\\"metrics token の text 出力を改修し、サマリー行を AVG 行としてカラム配置で表示。specs 列・cache hit 列追加、cost 精度変更、フェーズ区切りとヘッダー構造を改善する。\\\",\\nspecs/247-metrics-token-avg-row/draft.json:6:    \\\"proposedApproach\\\": \\\"formatText を改修し、カラムヘッダーを先頭に一度だけ出力、フェーズ区切りを -- phase --- 形式に、サマリーを AVG 行としてデータ行と同じカラム配置で出力。行データに specCount と cacheHitRate を追加。\\\",\\nspecs/247-metrics-token-avg-row/draft.json:7:    \\\"validation\\\": \\\"表示変更のみで集計ロジック（分母の混在）は変更しない。既存の `metrics.js` routes to `token` subcommand independently without requiring `check`.; P1 — R2 (read only specs/*/flow.json) — `listFlowFiles()` scans `specs/` directory for `flow.json` files only; no `.tmp/logs` path referenced.; P1 — R3 (--format text|json|csv, text default) — `DEFAULT_FORMAT = \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"`, `SUPPORTED_FORMATS = new Set([\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"json\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"csv\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"])`, all three renderers produce same aggregation fields.; P1 — R4 (text output: phase blocks, date rows, columns difficulty/tokenIn/tokenOut/cacheRead/cacheCreate/callCount/cost) — `formatText()` emits `PHASE <name>` headers, per-date rows, and all seven required columns.; P1 — R5 (missing fields render as N/A) — `createEmptyRow()` initializes all fields to `null`; `asDisplayValue(null)` returns `\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"N/A\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"`; processing continues.; P1 — R6 (runtime failure → non-zero exit) — `main().catch()` calls `process.exit(EXIT_ERROR)`; JSON parse errors, unreadable files, and cache write failures all throw and are caught.; P1 — R7 (5000 file bound, non-zero exit on overflow) — `MAX_FLOW_FILES = 5000`; `listFlowFiles()` throws when `files.length > MAX_FLOW_FILES`, which propagates to `EXIT_ERROR`.; P2 — R8 (cache freshness: regenerate when any flow.json newer than metrics.json) — `isCacheFresh()` compares cache mtime against the *latest* flow.json mtime, not against each individual file. If the cache was written after the newest flow.json, it returns true and reuses the cache. This is correct behavior for the spec's stated rule (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"newer than `.sdd-forge/output/metrics.json`\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"), so this is actually a PASS.; P2 — R8 (cache freshness check) — `isCacheFresh()` compares `cachePath` mtime against `latestFlowMtimeMs` (max of all flow.json mtimes); cache is regenerated when missing or older than any source file.\\\\\\\",\\\\n../specs/166-cache-hit-ratio-aggregation/flow.json:129:      \\\\\\\"desc\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"R8: metrics.json キャッシュの鮮度判定で再生成/再利用\\\\\\\",\\\\n../specs/166-cache-hit-ratio-aggregation/flow.json:174:      \\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"draft: metricsキャッシュ再生成は specs/*/flow.json と output/metrics.json のmtime比較のみ\\\\\\\",\\\\n../specs/239-retro-incomplete-reasons/issue.md:1:When retro rate < 1.0, append the requirement IDs and notes for partial/not_done items to the end of the Retro section in the report. 1. Unit test coverage is overlooked\\\\n**File:** `tests/unit/metrics/token-duration.test.js`  \\\\n**Issue:** This test imports `formatText`, `formatCsv`, and `buildRowsFromMetrics`, and asserts the old table/CSV behavior. The spec only mentions `token.test.js`, so this test will likely fail or miss new `specCount` / `cacheHitRate` behavior.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Add explicit spec/test tasks to update this unit test for the new global header, AVG row layout, CSV columns, cost precision, and `buildRowsFromMetrics` returning `specCount=1` plus `cacheHitRate`.\\\\n\\\\n### 2. Cache reuse test contradicts new cache-version behavior\\\\n**File:** `tests/e2e/metrics/token-finalized-at.test.js`  \\\\n**Issue:** The existing cache reuse test creates a cache with matching `maxFinalizedAt` and expects it to be reused as-is. With mandatory new row fields, reusing an unversioned old cache would omit `specCount` and `cacheHitRate`. R7 is only `should`, but the new fields are `must`.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Make cache version invalidation a `must`, and require tests where unversioned/mismatched-version cache rebuilds even when `maxFinalizedAt` matches. Update the reuse fixture to include the expected cache `version` and new row fields.\\\\n\\\\n### 3. Exported helper path is missing from the module list\\\\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \\\\n**Issue:** The spec lists `buildRows`, `createEmptyRow`, `applyPhaseMetrics`, and `finalizeRow`, but omits exported `buildRowsFromMetrics()`. That helper is used by tests and currently finalizes rows directly, so it must also produce `specCount` and `cacheHitRate`.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Add `buildRowsFromMetrics` to the Scope/Modules/Data Flow and specify its expected behavior for single-flow rows.\\\\n\\\\n### 4. Cache read path is underspecified\\\\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \\\\n**Issue:** The spec mentions `writeCache` and `isCacheFresh`, but not `readCacheRows`. If cache validation is incomplete, `readCacheRows` can still return old rows missing the new required fields.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Add a requirement that cached rows are only read after schema/version validation, or that `readCacheRows` rejects caches missing required row fields.\\\\n\\\\n### 5. Tracked generated cache artifact is not addressed\\\\n**File:** `.sdd-forge/output/metrics.json`  \\\\n**Issue:** This file is tracked and currently contains old row objects without `version`, `specCount`, or `cacheHitRate`. The spec changes the cache schema but does not say whether this artifact must be regenerated, updated, or intentionally left untouched.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Add an explicit decision for the tracked cache artifact: regenerate it with the new schema, remove it from the change scope, or document why it is excluded.\\\\n\\\\n### 6. Summary behavior across CSV/JSON is ambiguous\\\\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \\\\n**Issue:** `computePhaseSummary()` feeds both JSON `phaseSummary` and CSV summary rows, but R8 only says `avgSpecCount` and `avgDifficulty` are reflected in the AVG row. It does not clearly define whether CSV `SUMMARY` becomes `AVG`, whether CSV includes `avgSpecCount`, `avgDifficulty`, `avgCallCount`, or whether JSON `phaseSummary` must change.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Specify the summary contract per format: text AVG row columns, CSV summary row label/columns, and JSON `phaseSummary` fields including `avgCallCount`, `avgSpecCount`, `avgDifficulty`, and weighted `cacheHitRate`.\\\"}}\\nspecs/247-metrics-token-avg-row/draft-review.md:7:{\\\"type\\\":\\\"item.completed\\\",\\\"item\\\":{\\\"id\\\":\\\"item_0\\\",\\\"type\\\":\\\"agent_message\\\",\\\"text\\\":\\\"### 1. JSON/CSV scope is unsupported\\\\n**QA:** Q1  \\\\n**Issue:** The request is about table display changes, especially AVG row/header/phase delimiter. “全フォーマットに追加” expands scope without issue evidence.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Reframe as: “Are `specs` and `cache hit` required only in text output, or also JSON/CSV?” If no issue evidence exists, mark JSON/CSV as out of scope or explicitly require confirmation.\\\\n\\\\n### 2. Cache version answer is over-prescriptive\\\\n**QA:** Q2  \\\\n**Issue:** The QA jumps to an implementation design (`version` field) rather than clarifying required behavior. It also assumes old cache cannot support the new fields without proving cache contents.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Change the entry to specify the requirement: “Cached output must not omit newly added columns/fields.” Then separately note implementation options, with evidence from actual cache schema.\\\\n\\\\n### 3. AVG row formatting is underspecified\\\\n**QA:** Q3  \\\\n**Issue:** The answer lists values but does not define display formatting for `specs`, `difficulty`, token/cache counts, `cache hit`, `call count`, or `duration`. These are needed for a useful spec.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Add exact formatting rules per AVG column, including rounding, percent display for cache hit, blank/null behavior, and whether counts are integer-rounded or decimal averages.\\\\n\\\\n### 4. Cost precision conflicts across entries\\\\n**QA:** Q3/Q7  \\\\n**Issue:** Q3 says `avgCost($toFixed(1))`, while Q7 says text/CSV use `$0.4` strings and JSON stays numeric. The request says “cost精度をtoFixed(1)” but does not clearly limit this to AVG or text/CSV.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Split and clarify: “Which cost values are affected: data rows, AVG rows, CSV, JSON?” Then define exact behavior per output format.\\\\n\\\\n### 5. Phase delimiter width rule is ambiguous\\\\n**QA:** Q5  \\\\n**Issue:** “現行の罫線幅と同じ文字数” may conflict with the requested literal style `-- phase ---`. It does not specify padding, total width, or behavior for longer phase labels.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Define exact construction, e.g. prefix `-- `, phase label, space, trailing hyphens to table width, with a minimum suffix of `---`.\\\\n\\\\n### 6. Missing coverage for header and delimiter interaction\\\\n**QA:** NEW  \\\\n**Issue:** The draft says the header appears once and phase sections use delimiters, but it does not specify the exact output order.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Add QA for structure: header first, then each phase delimiter, rows, AVG row; no repeated header between phases.\\\\n\\\\n### 7. Redundant specs/cache-hit meaning\\\\n**QA:** Q1/Q6/Q8  \\\\n**Issue:** `specs` and `cache hit` are covered in multiple entries with overlapping but slightly different framing. This can create drift.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Keep Q8 as the semantic definition, keep Q6 for column placement, and remove or narrow Q1 to output-format scope only.\\\\n\\\\n### 8. AVG label is not justified\\\\n**QA:** Q3  \\\\n**Issue:** The answer uses `AVG.` with a period, while the issue says `AVG行` and does not prove the literal label includes a period.  \\\\n**Suggestion:** Confirm the exact label from the issue image or specify `AVG` unless the image explicitly shows `AVG.`.\\\"}}\\ntests/unit/flow/phases-review.test.js:4:const { VALID_PHASES } = 1. E2E Assertions Still Expect Old Text/CSV Shape\\n**File:** `tests/e2e/metrics/token.test.js`  \\n**Issue:** Existing tests assert `PHASE draft` and the old CSV header. The spec changes phase separators, single header placement, and adds `specCount/cacheHitRate`, but does not mention updating these tests.  \\n**Suggestion:** Add explicit test scope for updating E2E assertions for the new text phase separator, one-time header behavior, CSV header order, `specCount`, `cacheHitRate`, and `toFixed(1)` cost output.\\n\\n### 2. Unit Tests Cover Exported Formatter Contracts\\n**File:** `tests/unit/metrics/token-duration.test.js`  \\n**Issue:** This file imports `formatText`, `formatCsv`, and `buildRowsFromMetrics` directly. The spec mentions changing these functions but does not call out direct unit coverage that will break or need expansion.  \\n**Suggestion:** Add requirements to update unit tests for `buildRowsFromMetrics` returning `specCount/cacheHitRate`, text AVG row alignment, duration null display, and CSV cost precision.\\n\\n### 3. Cache Reuse Tests Conflict With Version Invalidation\\n**File:** `tests/e2e/metrics/token-finalized-at.test.js`  \\n**Issue:** One test currently expects a cache with matching `maxFinalizedAt` to be reused as-is. R7 requires a cache `version` field and rebuild on mismatch, so this behavior must change for old cache payloads.  \\n**Suggestion:** Specify that cache reuse now requires both matching `maxFinalizedAt` and expected cache version, and update tests for legacy cache with `maxFinalizedAt` but missing `version`.\\n\\n### 4. Tracked Cache Artifact Is Overlooked\\n**File:** `.sdd-forge/output/metrics.json`  \\n**Issue:** The repository includes a generated metrics cache with old row objects that lack `version`, `specCount`, and `cacheHitRate`. The spec changes the cache schema but does not say whether this tracked artifact should be regenerated or left stale.  \\n**Suggestion:** Add an explicit decision: regenerate `.sdd-forge/output/metrics.json` with the new schema, remove it from scope, or document why it is intentionally unchanged.\\n\\n### 5. `readCacheRows` Is Missing From Module Scope\\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \\n**Issue:** The module list names `writeCache` and `isCacheFresh`, but not `readCacheRows`. Since cached rows become part of the public output, stale cached rows without new fields can affect text/json/csv rendering.  \\n**Suggestion:** Include `readCacheRows` in the target function list and require cached rows to be read only after version validation, or normalized/rejected if required fields are absent.\\n\\n### 6. AVG Call Count Semantics Are Underspecified\\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \\n**Issue:** Constraints say `token/callCount` use null=0 with all-row average, but current `computePhaseSummary()` exposes `totalCalls`, and R8 only adds `avgSpecCount` and `avgDifficulty`. The AVG row’s `call count` column is ambiguous.  \\n**Suggestion:** Add `avgCallCount` to R8 and define whether CSV/JSON summaries keep `totalCalls`, add `avgCallCount`, or replace the summary value used for aligned AVG rows.\"}}\nspecs/247-metrics-token-avg-row/spec.json:2:  \"goal\": \"metrics token の出力を改修し、サマリー行を AVG 行としてテーブルカラムに揃えて表示する。specs 列・cache hit 列を全フォーマットに追加し、cost 精度を toFixed(1) に変更する。\",\nspecs/247-metrics-token-avg-row/spec.json:10:      \"[must] 全フォーマット: 行データに cacheHitRate (cacheRead / (tokenInput + cacheRead)) を追加\",\nspecs/247-metrics-token-avg-row/spec.json:30:      { Responsibility — メトリクス token の text 出力フォーマット改修という単一 concern に絞られており、無関係な変更は含まれていない。; Unambiguous Requirements — toFixed(1)、カラム順序、フェーズ区切り形式など、検証可能な条件で要件が記述されている。主観的形容詞の使用は最小限。; Complete Context — QA 各エントリに evidence・why・considered が揃い、独立した2人のレビュアーが実装の合否を判断できる水準に達している。; No Hardcoded Secrets — フォーマット変更のみの spec であり、シークレット類は一切含まれない。; Prioritize Requirements — scopeVerification.in に5項目の要件があるが、must / should / nice-to-have の優先度が一切割り当てられていない。3項目超の場合は優先度付けが必須。; Impact on Existing Features — impactOnExisting に3項目（外部ツール影響・token.test.js アサーション破損・CSV アサーション影響）が明示されている。; Present Recommendations with Reasoning — 全 QA エントリに推奨回答 + evidence（コード箇所引用）+ why + considered の構成が整っている。; Explore Before Asking — token.js の具体的な行番号（:113-146, :284-307, :159-161 等）を参照しており、コードを事前に読んで回答していることが確認できる。; Proactively Raise Related Implications — キャッシュスキーマ変更による旧キャッシュ不整合、e2e テスト破損、CSV アサーション影響など関連する波及を事前に列挙している。; Confirm Brainstorm vs. Decision Before Critiquing — approval.approved=true で承認済みの決定ドキュメントであり、ブレインストーム段階ではない。; Capture Digressions in draft.md — これは構造化された spec であり、会話上の脱線が発生する文脈ではないため評価不可。; Draft Stays at Requirements Level — キャッシュ再計算の QA 回答（answer フィールド）に「version フィールドを追加しコード側の期待 version と不一致ならリビルド」という内部データ構造・制御フローの記述が含まれている。answer フィールドは guardrail の除外対象（evidence/why/considered）ではなく、要件レベルの記述（例:「旧フォーマット検出時に自動再構築すること」）に留めるべき。; Backward-Compatible CLI Interface — CLI コマンド・オプションの変更はなく、変更対象は formatText の出力フォーマットのみ。マイグレーションプランの適用外。\",\nspecs/247-metrics-token-avg-row/draft.json:3:  \"goal\": \"metrics token の text 出力を改修し、サマリー行を AVG 行としてカラム配置で表示。specs 列・cache hit {"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_27","type":"agent_message","text":"### 1. CSV summary row schema is undefined\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \n**Issue:** `formatCsv` currently emits per-phase `SUMMARY` rows, but the spec only defines the AVG row for text. Adding `specCount` / `cacheHitRate` changes CSV column alignment, and it is unclear whether CSV summary rows remain `SUMMARY`, become `AVG.`, or include average values.  \n**Suggestion:** Add a requirement defining exact CSV header order and summary-row behavior, including values/placeholders for `specCount`, `cacheHitRate`, `difficulty`, cache columns, `callCount`, `cost`, `durationMs`, and `incomplete`.\n\n### 2. JSON `phaseSummary` shape is underspecified\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \n**Issue:** `formatJson` outputs `{ rows, phaseSummary }`, but the spec only says row data gets new fields and R8 only names `avgSpecCount` / `avgDifficulty`. It does not define whether `phaseSummary` keeps `totalCalls`, adds `avgCacheRead`, `avgCacheCreate`, `avgCallCount`, or how `cacheHitRate` is calculated there.  \n**Suggestion:** Add a JSON output contract for `phaseSummary`, explicitly listing fields, numeric/null behavior, and confirming JSON values remain numbers without display rounding.\n\n### 3. `cacheHitRate` display and null handling are ambiguous\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \n**Issue:** R5 defines the formula, but not how nullable `tokenInput` / `cacheRead` should be treated, nor whether text/CSV display should be raw decimal, percentage, or rounded percentage.  \n**Suggestion:** Specify that missing token/cache values are treated as `0` for the formula, `null` is used only when the denominator is `0`, JSON emits decimal number/null, and text/CSV use a defined display format such as `toFixed(0)%` or decimal.\n\n### 4. Exported test helper is omitted from scope\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \n**Issue:** The spec lists `buildRows`, `createEmptyRow`, `applyPhaseMetrics`, and `finalizeRow`, but omits exported `buildRowsFromMetrics`, which unit tests use to construct rows. If only `buildRows` handles `specCount`, helper-generated rows may miss `specCount` / `cacheHitRate`.  \n**Suggestion:** Add `buildRowsFromMetrics` to the affected modules/functions and require it to return rows with `specCount: 1` and computed `cacheHitRate`.\n\n### 5. Cache-version requirement conflicts with existing e2e fixture\n**File:** `tests/e2e/metrics/token-finalized-at.test.js`  \n**Issue:** The existing cache reuse test writes a cache with matching `maxFinalizedAt` but no `version`, then expects reuse. R7 requires rebuilding when the expected cache version is missing/mismatched.  \n**Suggestion:** Add test updates to the spec: reuse only when `version` matches; rebuild when `version` is absent/mismatched even if `maxFinalizedAt` matches; assert rebuilt cache contains `version`, `specCount`, and `cacheHitRate`.\n\n### 6. Existing text/CSV assertions are not covered\n**File:** `tests/e2e/metrics/token.test.js`  \n**Issue:** Current tests assert `PHASE draft` and the old CSV header. The spec changes phase separators, one-time headers, and CSV columns, but does not mention updating or adding coverage.  \n**Suggestion:** Add acceptance criteria for updating e2e tests to assert `-- draft ---`, one global text header, `AVG.` row alignment, `specs` / `cache hit` columns, and CSV header/order.\n\n### 7. Design principle contradicts required data/cache changes\n**File:** `src/metrics/commands/token.js`  \n**Issue:** “表示フォーマットの変更のみ” conflicts with adding row fields, changing JSON/CSV output, and adding cache schema versioning. These are data-model/cache contract changes, not only display formatting.  \n**Suggestion:** Reword the principle to: existing metric aggregation semantics stay unchanged, but the derived row/output model and cache schema may change for `specCount`, `cacheHitRate`, and cache invalidation."}}
